In base-9, 13 is represented as \(14_9\) (since \(13 \div 9 = 1\) remainder \(4\)).\n     - Write down 4 and carry over 1.\n     - The leftmost digit is 1 from the carry.\n\nThus, the sum of \(81_9\) and \(55_9\) is \(146_9\).\n\nTherefore, the result of \(73 + 50\) in base-9 is \(\boxed{146_9}\).

We do this by dividing by powers of 9:\n\n1. Determine the largest power of 9 less than or equal to 108. Divide 108 by 81:\n   \[\n   108 \div 81 = 1 \quad \text{remainder} \quad 108 - 81 = 27\n   \]\n   So, the coefficient for \(9^2\) is 1.\n\n3.
